* `Strategy`: - ((Strategy + Plan - <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Taktik</span>) = Rigid Inflexibility). You have a goal and a schedule, but you don't know how to actually execute the maneuvers on the ground.
* `Strategy`: - ((<span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Taktik</span> + Plan - Strategy) = Busy Work). You are doing lots of things efficiently and on schedule, but you have no idea why or if it's actually helping you win.

* `Kompakt`: - Kompakt fine, dry loose SiltSoil before [Spade.Ingress] to ensure that the Soil around the <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span> will be dense enough to support the hole otherwise when you Systole the <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span> only the soil that was in the <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span> will be compressed and will be a loose tree fault.

* `NormalForce`: - To prevent soil collapse below <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span>
* `Jerk`: - Deeper Ingress by increasing LocalMass MassDistribution when you're already Leaning over onto your Spade. You can do a 30mm Slam like a OneInchPunch. Deepen the <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span> and [Dilate.Pry] [Dilate.Collapse]
* `Ritmo.md`: - (Dilate,Insert) before <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span> Collapses
* `Egression`: - Seedling Ingress <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span>
* `Egression`: - Neon Ingress <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span>

* `Egression`: - Hand Ingress <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Hole</span>

## Neosite
* `Kontakt-Spade-Shaft`: - Used when pushing and holding Flora back away from <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - The zone where a <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span> can go relative to PlantedSites
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - The side lengths of the <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>Polygon.
* `Mikrosite-Select`: - You don't select a <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>, a <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span> is a Mikrosite which has already been planted
* `Neon`: - "<span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>" doesn't make sense, a planted Neon is a Neon. Neons in your Bag aren't instantiated. Besides the weight of Bags, there is no experience of a Neon unless it's in your hand or you're looking at it already planted in the land. There should never be any dropped trees to be confusing. A Mikrosite which has been planted is no longer a Mikrosite for another Neon.
* `Mikrosite`: - <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span> doesn't include Natural Crop Trees
* `Mikrosite`: - <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span> is a planted Mikrosite
* `ViaOverview`: - Abandon CurrentSite for <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span> when you determine that CurrentSite isn't good
* `Triad`: - Triad of <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>s, Stumps, Protektors
* `RelativeDirection`: - <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>
* `RelativeDirection`: - <span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>.Markum
* `RelativeDirection`: - (Follow = RelativeTo(<span style="background-color: lime; color: black; font-weight: bold;">Neosite</span>.Markum))
